The owner's 2026-09-15 standing direction requires every future **Souls and
Simulacra** recommendation to assess *Achintya Bheda Abheda* fit at a named
operative relationship and boundary. Meaningful continuity or participation
and real, mechanically legible distinction should coexist there; separate
entries that merely distribute “both” do not satisfy the ideal. Each
recommendation must rate the fit as direct, partial, neutral, or aggregate and
expose the gameplay tradeoff. This is an inspirational analogy, not theological
equivalence, a silent invariant, or authority to bypass prerequisites, supply
unstated rules, reopen directions, or select for the owner. An incompatible
option may remain visible as a real alternative but may not be described as
satisfying the ideal; if no direction remains consistent, the card must add or
recommend an explicit replacement rather than weaken the north star.

## Standard applied

A direction receives a substantive `PASS` only when the durable record shows:

1. bounded alternatives or an owner-written replacement path;
2. a recommendation and real tradeoff;
3. a concrete exploit, fault, or boundary case;
4. prerequisite-correct ordering; and
5. an owner response limited to the exposed semantics.

For a closed decision, the record must additionally contain complete normative
wording and an explicit closing disposition. The owner's 2026-09-11 workflow
correction now requires future choices to be presented one at a time. Earlier
grouped rounds can still pass substantively when every constituent choice was
individually exposed before the grouped response; their cadence is reported
rather than rewritten.

`PROVENANCE GAP` means the current rule was explicitly accepted but durable
repository history cannot prove that each independent product choice received
the later A/B/C treatment before the complete replay. It does not mean the
conversation did not occur or silently revoke an accepted rule.

| --- | --- | --- |

| EP-D02 | **PROVENANCE GAP; accepted rule remains authoritative** | The complete eighteen-clause [replacement](endless-progression-decisions.md#ep-d02--personal-rule-capacity-grows-to-a-finite-48-load-ceiling) and explicit acceptance are durable. The preceding [handoff](../handoffs/2026-09-03-0850--chat-close-next-ep-d02-variable-rule-load.md#recommended-opening-round-for-the-next-agent) still called multiple source, ownership, accounting, eligibility, cadence, and migration choices unresolved; Git has no intermediate decision-tree record before the one complete accepted replay. |
| EP-D07, including dropout supplement | **PROVENANCE GAP; accepted rule remains authoritative** | The complete [base and corrected dropout rules](endless-progression-decisions.md#ep-d07--mode-neutral-gladiators-and-human-only-allied-mvp), alternatives, boundary tests, withdrawn defective replay, and explicit acceptances are durable. The proposal, alternatives, and acceptance entered history together, so the repository does not prove prior atomic A/B/C treatment of every identity, admission, disconnect, authority, and settlement choice. |

EP-D02 and EP-D07 may change only through a complete explicitly accepted
replacement. A confirmation pass can establish the requested decision rigor
while leaving each current clause authoritative unless the owner actually
selects a revision.

### Minimal EP-D02 confirmation queue

The independent choices whose granular pre-acceptance provenance is not durable
are, in dependency order:

1. personal versus campaign/party Capacity ownership — **A confirmed on
   2026-09-11; current accepted personal direction retained**;
2. simultaneous Legendary-bearing item-host grammar — **A confirmed on
   2026-09-11; current accepted sixteen-host direction retained**;
3. the corrected standalone/non-item cluster:
   - **C3a — Bound Soul combat-source role: A confirmed on 2026-09-11; current
     accepted gear-independent role retained**;
   - **C3b — Forms/Signatures/Keystones placement and v1 admission as one
     legacy-vocabulary family: A confirmed on 2026-09-11; current accepted
     item-contained-only placement retained**; split it further if their
     treatment diverges;
   - **C3c — Charm/Soul Relic placement: owner replacement direction selected
     on 2026-09-11**; design both outside item hosts while leaving release
     timing open;
   - **C3c.1 — relationship topology on the dedicated Charm/Soul Relic surface
     outside item hosts: C selected on 2026-09-11**; active Soul Relics are roots
     and every active Charm is a dependent customizer;
   - **C3c.2 — Soul Relic root gameplay purpose: A selected on 2026-09-12**;
     useful output requires a cross-source relationship among multiple
     independently legal and already-accounted sources;
   - **C3c.3 — Relic-to-Bound-Soul relationship: B selected on 2026-09-12**;
     every pair remains legal and selects a qualitative grammar variant without
     a scalar bonus or automatic source;
   - **C3c.4 — Charm customization authority: C selected on 2026-09-12**; the
     complete catalog must support materially functional input-type and
     payoff-type Charms, while each individual Charm is statically one-sided;
   - **Codex correction, 2026-09-12:** the former next node
     “ownership/persistence” bundled distinct decisions and also grouped the
     Relic root with its dependent Charms. It is withdrawn. **C3c.5 — Soul Relic
     access scope was introduced** to ask only whose access state makes an
     otherwise-legal Relic a configuration candidate. Its revised card
     compares mutually exclusive `combatantId`, `campaignId`, and new global
     `playerProfileId` access keys; the earlier typed personal/campaign
     alternative was withdrawn before selection. The owner's compatible
     individual-earning proposal is a separate next choice rather than a
     silently selected premise;
   - **C3c.5 — Soul Relic access scope: A selected on 2026-09-12**; one
     exclusive `combatantId` collection makes a Relic a candidate, with no
     campaign/profile fallback and no acquisition or instance rule;
   - **C3c.6 — Charm access scope: A selected on 2026-09-12**; both Charm types
     consult only the `combatantId` collection, independently of the required
     personal Relic root;
   - **C3c.7 — Soul Relic possession unit: A selected on 2026-09-12**; every
     candidate requires one unique instance record while it exists, without
     inheriting the ordinary-item state machine or permitting duplicates,
     simultaneous use, transfer, or loss;
   - **C3c.8 — Charm possession unit: A selected on 2026-09-12**; both Charm
     types use authoritative per-unit IDs, and mechanically identical instances
     never collapse into a quantity or membership entry;
   - possession remains split by component because
     the Relic-root/Charm-customizer relationship does not require both
     components to use one possession unit;
   - **C3c.9 — zero-Soul-Relic product status: C selected on 2026-09-12**; the
     first legality-only card was withdrawn after the owner correctly challenged
     its implicit power meaning. The revision distinguishes a legal omission
     with no parity promise, a competitively supported mutually exclusive
     no-Relic archetype,
     and the selected eventual mandatory root after a non-stranding enablement
     gate;
   - **C3c.10 — maximum active Soul Relic roots: A selected on 2026-09-12**;
     after safe enablement exactly one personal Relic instance is active, while
     inactive owned alternatives provide no grammar, resonance, payoff, or
     Charm authority. This result makes simultaneous same-definition duplicate
     activation structurally illegal, so the dependent activation question
     collapses rather than receiving a vacuous card;
   - **C3c.11 — same-definition Soul Relic possession: A selected on
     2026-09-12**; a legal combatant collection holds at most one instance per
     stable definition, and an operation may not commit a second holding;
   - **C3c.12 — same-definition Soul Relic duplicate-award destination: A
     selected on 2026-09-12**; before its declared reward pool is complete, an
     otherwise-earned duplicate substitutes an otherwise-eligible unowned
     definition from that pool and creates neither infusion nor currency;
   - **C3c.13 — duplicate-award substitute selection authority: B selected on
     2026-09-12**; the receiving player makes the final choice whenever at least
     two substitute candidates are presented, while one candidate resolves
     automatically. The first draft remains withdrawn because it bundled
     authority with full-versus-sampled breadth, fixed draft cardinality,
     weighted sampling, and a pool-size requirement;
   - **C3c.14 — duplicate-substitute offer breadth topology: B selected on
     2026-09-12**; one fixed bounded-subset rule applies to every offer, while
     exact size and sampling remain separate;
   - **C3c.15 — duplicate-substitute offer size: B selected on 2026-09-12**;
     each offer contains three distinct candidates when at least three remain,
     all candidates when two remain, and the sole automatic result when one
     remains;
   - **C3c.16 — duplicate-substitute sampling measure: A selected on
     2026-09-12**; every source preserves its later positive definition weights
     after eligibility/ownership conditioning, and at least one reachable
     source must be materially nonuniform. The first card remains withdrawn
     because its third option bundled a separate personal-history adjustment;
   - **C3c.17 — duplicate-substitute presentation-history protection: A
     selected on 2026-09-12**; prior presentations and declines never alter
     later candidate likelihood, so no dependent history-state branch opens;
   - **C3c.18 — complete-pool Relic reward destination: A selected on
     2026-09-12**; an otherwise-earned Relic event with no eligible-unowned
     definition atomically reroutes through a versioned nonempty fallback in
     the source's existing non-Relic economy and creates no Relic item, mastery
     receipt, or dedicated Relic currency;
   - **C3c.19 — duplicate-substitute display order: B selected on
     2026-09-12**; every permutation of the two or three displayed candidates
     is equally likely, commits with the offer, and survives replay of that
     settlement unchanged;
   - **C3c.20 — Soul Relic legal custody scope: A selected on 2026-09-12**; every
     created instance has exactly one current `ownerCombatantId`, including
     while in personal claim escrow, without yet forbidding later transfer;
   - **C3c.21 — Soul Relic owner-side action authority: A selected on
     2026-09-12**; only the owning combatant's persistent custodian may approve
     the owner side of later-permitted discretionary Relic mutations;
   - **C3c.22 — voluntary Soul Relic transfer scope: A selected on
     2026-09-12**; title never changes beneficiary through a voluntary player
     action, so consent, exchange, and transfer-horizon branches collapse;
   - **C3c.23 — persistent Soul Relic loss severity: A selected on
     2026-09-12**; ordinary gameplay can cause no persistent impairment or

     suppression and explicit lifecycle remain separate;
   - **Derived Relic activation lane:** the dedicated non-item surface,
     personal collection, mandatory post-enablement lower bound, and one-root

     storage, accounting, and Bound Soul hosting remain unselected;

   - **C3c.32 — minimum evidence required for a counted source: B selected on

   - **C3c.34 — independently attributable evidence-form support: C selected on

   - **Codex C82 frontier-order result, 2026-09-16:** C71/C72/C73/C80 stabilize

   - **Codex C83 frontier-order result, 2026-09-16:** define

   - **C3c.151 — same-decisive-cut dual-form participation: A selected**; for each

   - **Codex correction, 2026-09-14:** the first C3c.43 simultaneous-channel
     draft was withdrawn before owner presentation and selected nothing.
     Internal result slots can collapse to one aggregate behavior; preventing
     that collapse depended on silently selecting player-disclosed recipe
     identities and separately observable progress/results. Resolve that

     pre-payoff consumer result identities, with payoff-only data projected out;
   - **Codex factorization correction, 2026-09-14:** C3c.44-A's common
     equivalence partition still cannot canonically say whether `P+Q` is one
     categorical result or two co-occurring receipts. C3c.45 therefore makes a
     complete, versioned, positive semantic receipt family part of the authored
     player/consumer contract before classifying reachable co-occurrence. Raw
     Boolean re-encodings, complements, dormant bits, and extensionally
     co-occurring aliases cannot change that topology;
   - **Codex coverage-domain correction, 2026-09-14:** C3c.43's operative
     realization domain requires a material predicate portfolio but not a
     positive result receipt after projection. C3c.47 therefore restricts its
     denominator to realizations with at least one anchored `H+` context.

     operability branch and cannot masquerade as focused-only complements;
   - **Codex fixed-version correction, 2026-09-14:** the first C3c.47 draft
     treated every globally supported definition as though it had a nonempty

     definitions with an operative realization in `v`; a definition absent from
     that version cannot satisfy a focused, bearing, or silent negative class.
     C3c.47-A's equality over the receipt-bearing domain is unchanged;
   - **Codex frontier correction, 2026-09-14:** after C3c.47-A, positive-result
     operability and a receipt-bearing-only capability map are independent ready
     siblings. C3c.48 resolves operability first so the later system-wide map
     covers every materially operative realization rather than preserving an

   - **Codex frontier result, 2026-09-14:** C3c.48-A closes the receipt-silent

   - **Codex frontier result, 2026-09-14:** C3c.49-A fixes the full nonempty

   - **Codex frontier result, 2026-09-14:** C3c.50-A fixes that empty-input

   - **Codex frontier result, 2026-09-14:** C3c.51-A fixes the cell projection
     of the empty-input grounding relation to all six cells but does not fix

   - **Codex frontier result, 2026-09-14:** C3c.52-B fixes a nonempty cell

The next audit found that `RCS-03C2` still combined invocation-control source

An eleventh prerequisite audit then found that old `RCS-03C3B2A` compressed
five independent policies. Within one accepted multi-application operation,
semantic applications may share one root or split into several. Across
separately accepted operations, roots may remain separate or merge into a
finite authored episode. On the nonmerged complement, a compound order/
trajectory claim may retain only its full operand lineage or additionally own
an additive multi-parent relation root. These three policies vary
independently. After applicable action roots exist, a material nonterminating
revision may preserve or succeed a current state's root, while irreducibly
multi-root current-state support is a separate cardinality topology.
Founder-versus-successor and singleton-versus-composite support therefore
cannot share one option list.

One order/trajectory rule inside `H` is forced: a concrete compound claim
retains the nonempty union of all indispensable operand lineages and drops no
endpoint. Otherwise a wrapper could hide an ineligible origin or count an
already-accounted source twice. Whether the claim additionally owns a
higher-order relation root is B2A2B; atom addressability, exact operator, and
witness-tuple catalogs remain later.

The register makes `RCS-03C3B2A` a non-counting parent for five counted
children: within-operation root partition, cross-operation finite-episode
merging, additive compound-H relation-root promotion, current-state root

The owner then selected `RCS-03C3B2A1-A`. `S^{split}_v` is empty: every
accepted operation occurrence has one primitive pre-episode action-root
lineage across all authored applications it actually realizes. Distinct
applications may later become addressable children, but retain that lineage
and cannot occupy independent-source positions together. This preserves one
operative identity without erasing its meaningful manifestations, prevents a

`RCS-03C3B2A2A` is now the sole active card. Let `F^{xop}_v` be prospectively
source-authored, finite, disclosed multi-operation episode forms whose
membership and stop/restart grammar are canonical no later than the first
member's commitment. Let `M^{merge}_v` contain forms whose realized member operations
canonicalize to one episode root while retaining their complete primitive
ancestry. Raw causal connectedness, adjacency, a shared record, or a Relic
match cannot create membership. Each versioned form uses one total,
occurrence-stable policy: all realized members share one episode root or none
coarsen; partial/contextual variants require separate prospective form keys.
Every post-terminal or post-abort re-initiation opens a fresh episode identity.
The exhaustive options are no forms merge, every form merges, or a nonempty
proper subset merges.

B is recommended: every qualifying source episode becomes one canonical root.
It gives one teachable rule, prevents an integral multi-button lifecycle from
self-supplying several Relic roots, and directly combines distinct operation
histories with one continuing episode identity. The cost is loss of internal
cross-source eligibility and the danger of an overbroad episode catalog. A
keeps maximum action-level buildcraft but permits lifecycle root farming; C
adds a source-density taxonomy and authoring arbitrage. Conditional B2A2B still
owns additive relation-root promotion on the eligible nonmerged complement.

At presentation, the register contained 30 `SCREEN`, 1 `OWNER-OPEN`, 7

`Phi_SR = 31`.

The `r_A`,`r_B`/higher-order `r_T` countermodel preserves both ancestries and
overlap exclusion while changing later root-level behavior against unrelated

The owner then selected `RCS-03C3B2A2A-B`.
`M^{merge}_v=F^{xop}_v`, with at least one reachable functional witness:

than additive-root semantics remain enforced. This directly preserves
meaningful operational difference within one authored episode identity and
reduces `Phi_SR` from 31 to 30.

The worlds differ in readiness, interruption, and counterplay while preserving

The missing boundary is therefore player-material content support, not catalog

B1 moves from `OWNER-OPEN` to `DIR-SELECTED`, and conditional B2 moves from

The owner then selected `RCS-03C3B2A2B2-B`.

The selected rule creates no addressability, nested-operand, same-cut cascade,

A thirteenth prerequisite audit then disproved the old B2A3 row's assumption

Old `RCS-03C3B2A3` is therefore a non-counting parent for

Replacing one counted row with two adds one potential slot. The then-current

A3A moves from `OWNER-OPEN` to `DIR-SELECTED`, and conditional A3B moves from

The A3B prerequisite audit found no further split once succession is defined
as the one canonical current-state episode identity at the committed cut. For
each eligible revision occurrence, there is exactly one operative continuity
root before and after. Preservation keeps the root. Succession atomically
creates one fresh replacement root while the predecessor remains complete
historical ancestry rather than an automatically exposed second current
support candidate. At the revision cut, both policies append to the post-cut
root's cut-indexed lineage the deduplicated union of complete pre-cut ancestry
and every indispensable revision-cause lineage. Earlier snapshots and settled
results retain their earlier lineage; no later revision rewrites them. A4 still
owns the exact causal-support topology of the current fact.

The stable successor-form subset of nonempty `E^{state-rev}_v` is necessarily
empty, universal, or nonempty and proper. A makes every form preserve, B makes
every form succeed, and C requires reachable preserving and succeeding forms
under prospective disclosed classification. Occurrence-sensitive succession
based on Relic, target, payoff, favorable history, claim state, or present
recipe completion is invalid; a meaningful contextual variant needs its own
form.

A is recommended. A3A-B already makes evolving states materially different;
keeping one continuity root throughout one uninterrupted episode prevents
revision cadence, stack oscillation, material refresh, or mode cycling from
becoming hidden root currency. Heat may progress from `Heat I` to `Heat II` to
`White-Hot` under one `r_K`, with genuinely different predicates and counters.
This gives the root/manifestation relation the strongest direct local ideal
fit. B instead creates a fresh successor at every committed revision, enabling
distinct root-level milestones only if later access rules expose them but
creating root-chain, UI, persistence, and latent outward-reuse pressure. It
grants no response window. C reserves succession for named same-state-form
metamorphoses but adds a premium root-topology taxonomy.

Every branch keeps the episode uninterrupted and obeys the same exploit
guards. Failed, interrupted, preempted, and speculative revisions create no
successor. State content, operative-root pointer, and post-cut ancestry commit
or roll back atomically. Retry/replay of one canonical committed occurrence
returns the same root identity; reads, restore, logs, aliases, callbacks, and
Relic matches never duplicate it. Oscillation never resurrects an older root.
A no-op cannot qualify. All same-episode changes committed at one authoritative
cut canonicalize into one pre-to-post revision; distinct revisions require
distinct cuts, and no same-cut recursive promotion is allowed. Genuine
termination and re-establishment opens a new episode outside A3B; an internal
clear/rewrite without an authoritative absent cut cannot fake one. Succession
alone creates no H fact, refreshes no H age, breaks/rearms no readiness
episode, satisfies no tag, and exposes neither root. Preserve-plus-additive-root
is not a fourth A3B policy. No branch grants root/child addressability, nested
use, fan-out, cross-combatant access, claim, consumption, reuse, payoff,
persistence, or release authority. A4 follows every answer.

The owner selected `RCS-03C3B2A3B-A`. `Q^{state-succ}_v` is empty: every
successfully committed eligible revision preserves the founder continuity root
throughout the uninterrupted episode. Its cut-indexed lineage append-only adds
every indispensable revision-cause lineage, but no revision mints an additive
root. Distinct manifestations remain materially real; they simply share one
causal identity. This selection changes no response-window, H, readiness, tag,
addressability, claim/reuse, payoff, persistence, release, or implementation
rule.

A3B moves from `OWNER-OPEN` to `DIR-SELECTED`, and A4 moves from `SCREEN` to
`OWNER-OPEN`. The then-current fifty-six-slot register had 27 `SCREEN`, 1
`OWNER-OPEN`, 7 `PRUNED`, 18 `DIR-SELECTED`, 1 `DERIVED`, 1 `SPEC`, and 1
`EVALUATE`; `Phi_SR = 28`. `RCS-03C3B2A4` was the sole active card.

The A4 prerequisite audit separates the episode's one continuity identity from
the root cardinality of the fact's presently operative causal support. For a
canonical current-state fact `f` at authoritative cut `t`, let
`B^{support}_t(f)` be its family of inclusion-minimal direct support bases.
Each basis is a nonempty set of already-existing canonical roots after the
selected operation and episode coarsening and applicable relation promotion.
A qualifying multi-root basis requires at least two members that are presently
co-necessary for the same fact, have disjoint complete lineages, have no
ancestor relation, and share no nonmultiplying authorization/accounting parent.
A reachable matched witness must hold form, source roles, read H, and every
non-support input fixed while removing any one member changes the fact's truth,
semantic content, legal continuation, or authoritative outcome. No proper
subset may suffice.

This definition defeats the relevant countermodels. A relation root with
multi-parent ancestry is one direct member, not several; it cannot count beside
an ancestor. Aliases and semantic children carrying one root do not multiply
support. Roots that take turns across cuts are not simultaneous. Alternative
singleton bases `{r_A}` and `{r_B}` do not become conjunctive `{r_A,r_B}` merely
because both are present. A conjunctive route beside singleton `r_C` qualifies
only in a reachable context where `r_C` is absent or ineffective and every
member of the conjunctive basis is indispensable. Adding a revision cause to
the preserved continuity root's ancestry does not make it current support.

Let `E^{state-mroot}_v` contain the prospectively versioned canonical
current-state source forms with at least one reachable, committed,
non-preempted, materially functional occurrence having such a basis. A4 has
exactly two admitted alternatives: A makes this set empty; B makes it nonempty.
B is recommended as an existential support floor. It permits at least one
composite state in which distinct roots directly sustain one present fact—the
strongest local ideal fit at this boundary—while ordinary root-atomic states
remain legal. A is the clearest support and disruption grammar but excludes
that signature composite-state play.

An all-forms-versus-mixed third option fails the closure-index admission test.
It changes only catalog saturation after support is admitted, repeats the
anti-recursion rule's barred some-to-all pattern, and does not change an
occurrence-level legal or counterplay contract: a universally capable form may
still have root-atomic occurrences. Exact forms, widths, counts, distribution,
prevalence, redundant alternatives, and incidence therefore remain
AUTHOR/SPEC unless later evidence meets the reopening test.

A4 mints no root, changes no continuity identity, and exposes no support member.
It creates no H/readiness/tag/payoff event. Loss of an indispensable member must
atomically select another already-valid minimal basis or commit the authored
revision/termination; a unique broken basis cannot leave a zombie fact.
Circular reference alone cannot prove an A4 witness: the qualifying basis must
be grounded by roots whose operative status is independent of the candidate
fact. A4 does not decide whether later-authored support graphs may contain
cyclic continuation. Root/child addressability, multi-position use, nesting,
fan-out, cross-combatant access, claims, consumption, reuse, payoff,
persistence, serialization, release, and implementation remain downstream.
B2B follows every answer.

The owner selected `RCS-03C3B2A4-B`. `E^{state-mroot}_v` is nonempty: at
least one prospectively versioned current-state source form must have a
reachable, committed, non-preempted, materially functional occurrence with an
inclusion-minimal direct support basis containing at least two independent
roots. The fact remains one continuity identity and one Relic position. Its
independent roots directly sustain one present manifestation without losing
their distinction, a direct local ideal fit. This is only an existential floor;
ordinary root-atomic states remain legal and exact breadth remains AUTHOR/SPEC.
A4 mints and exposes no root or downstream use.

Before further correction, A4 moved from `OWNER-OPEN` to `DIR-SELECTED` and

register with 26 `SCREEN`, 1 `OWNER-OPEN`, 7 `PRUNED`, 19 `DIR-SELECTED`, 1

The B2B prerequisite audit then found a support-before-equivalence defect.
C3c.31-A guarantees pure-build entries and C3c.32-B requires contingent combat
evidence, but neither proves that one exact build-provenance signature ever
spans two otherwise-distinct roots after the selected B2A rules. A legal
empty-domain world uses a bearer Bastion source and allied Ember source once
each. A legal nonempty-domain world adds two separately accepted and accounted
deeds from one exact configured Bastion origin, retaining distinct post-B2A
roots. A root-equivalence policy is vacuous in the former and changes legal
repeat-source density in the latter.

This is not an additive-build-root choice. The existing scope guard already
forbids an action view, build view, alias, packet, field, or resolver step from
minting another root. B2A2B2 alone owns additive compound-H relation roots, and
A4 alone owns multi-root support for one current-state fact. Nor may B2B split
one A4 fact into several positions or merge its simultaneous supporters merely
because they share that fact.

Old B2B is therefore a non-counting parent for support child B2B1 and
conditional equivalence child B2B2. Replacing one row with two raises the
register to fifty-seven slots: 27 `SCREEN`, 1 `OWNER-OPEN`, 7 `PRUNED`, 19
`DIR-SELECTED`, 1 `DERIVED`, 1 `SPEC`, and 1 `EVALUATE`; `Phi_SR = 28`.
B2B1 is the sole active card.

For B2B1, `Pi(x)` is the prospective exact build-provenance signature of a
contingent evidence occurrence offered to a pure-build position. It retains
source combatant, frozen legal configuration revision, every indispensable
exact configured causal origin, and each origin's configuration-side ownership
and custody provenance. It excludes occurrence identity and occurrence-specific
authorization, payment, and accounting lineages; those remain separate when
`Pi` is equal. Definitions and tags may be predicate attributes but cannot
replace exact causal origin.
Shared immutable provenance attribution or repeat-enabling equip entitlement
is metadata, not a member of either root's causal lineage or a nonmultiplying
authorization/accounting parent. It cannot itself authorize or pay for an
occurrence; reuse of one accepted authorization/payment event disqualifies the
pair.
The precise eligible family—item instance, payload, Technique,
component-owned occurrence, multi-component record, or another legal source—
remains AUTHOR/SPEC inside that invariant.

A positive repeated-provenance witness contains, at one authoritative
evaluation cut, two committed contingent evidence occurrences with equal
`Pi`, distinct roots after every B2A canonicalization, disjoint complete
lineages, separate authorization/accounting, and material pure-build candidate
use after Relic/payoff/claim/addressability projection. Aliases, phase or child
views, multiple fields/targets/hits/callbacks, one paid operation, configuration
alone, or two roots already coarsened upstream do not qualify.

Candidate admission is the immediate authoritative consequence. After the
second occurrence, the pure-build source ledger either may or may not contain
both same-`Pi` roots as simultaneous candidate records. This changes required
content/proof and the input offered to B2B2/B3 without granting position use;
the eventual source explanation must make the difference inspectable, while
exact UI remains downstream.

B2B1 has exactly two support alternatives. A makes
`E^{build-repeat}_v` empty. B makes it nonempty and is recommended: one
meaningful configured provenance may participate through several distinct
candidate manifestations, preserving repeat-source design space and making a
substantive later identity question. This is a partial local ideal fit until
B2B2 decides whether those roots remain distinct or coarsen. B itself grants
no repeat-source build, power, or position use. A is neutral/protective and
requires any later rule needing two independently sourced candidates to cross
exact provenance signatures. Empty versus nonempty is exhaustive; universal
prevalence is AUTHOR/SPEC.

The boundary counterexample fixes `Bastion#17` provenance and has Aster
separately authorize and pay for one Guard occurrence at `t_1` and another at
`t_3`, each tied to a different defended attack. Both facts remain admissible
at one evaluation cut and have distinct post-B2A roots and occurrence-accounting
lineages but the same exact provenance signature. Under A, the pure-build
source ledger cannot admit both as a same-provenance candidate pair; under B,
at least one form must admit both. B2B1 neither coarsens them nor permits
position occupancy. B2B2 decides remain-distinct versus coarsen for build
identity; even a remain-distinct answer grants no position use until B3.
Different combatants, owners, configuration
revisions, or exact causal origins never share `Pi` merely through equal
definition, tag, action, or presentation.

B2B1 mints, promotes, coarsens, and exposes no root. Every occurrence retains
origin, authorization, payment/accounting, phase, semantic content, and full
lineage; H freshness and S termination are unchanged. Upgrades, reforges,
unequip/re-equip, cross-encounter continuity, persistence, and migration remain
outside this fixed-configuration card. Root/child addressability, child
partition, nesting, fan-out, cross-combatant access, claims, consumption,
reuse, payoff, UI realization, release, and implementation remain downstream.
If B1-B is selected, B2B2 follows. If B1-A is selected, B2B2 is pruned and B3
follows.

Atomic/idempotent receipts, whole-state equip refusal, the arithmetic 48 ceiling
after its source/tariff choices, removal of the incompatible maximum-one
3-Load-identity rule, and EP-A03's fixed-four supersession are enforcement or
dependency consequences rather than extra owner choices.

### Minimal EP-D07 confirmation queue

The independent choices whose granular pre-acceptance provenance is not durable
are, in dependency order:

1. mode-neutral versus format-bound persistent identity;
2. Circuit participation/roster commitment horizon;
3. allied-human versus allied-AI admission;
4. one distinct human per seat versus human multi-seat control;
5. pause/original-custodian restoration versus seat succession after disconnect;
6. reconnect-only wait, automatic timeout, or team-initiated suspended exit;
7. absent-member authorization and grace timing;
8. connected-member voting threshold; and
9. ordinary Concede/Recovery versus a special dropout settlement.

Exactly-once action completion, terminal settlement, stale-vote invalidation,
atomic reconnect/abandon ordering, authenticated liveness, and original-roster
restoration are consistency/correctness consequences after those choices, not
additional product forks. Exact transport, heartbeat, duration, storage, and
transaction machinery remain unimplemented inputs.

### Minimal R9.4 repair

R9.1–R9.3 remain selected. Ask these in order, one at a time, skipping dependent

   binding claim, or frozen claim-class unions;
3. **R9.4c — total unresolved-mass ceiling:** exact percentage; and
4. **R9.4d — conditional binding-stratum ceiling:** exact percentage or none.

Unresolved terminals are never silently excluded, renormalized, or classified
as ties under selected R7/R8. A binding derived aggregate whose propagated
interval remains unresolved fails; that follows from R8.2 rather than creating
a fifth product choice.

## Sections not yet accepted

EP-D03, EP-D04, EP-D05, EP-D06, EP-A01, EP-A02, and EP-A03 remain pending in
the [authoritative record](endless-progression-decisions.md#decision-summary).
The owner packet now labels D03/D05/D06 prefilled answers as recommendations
that are pending and unselected, eliminating wording that looked like closure
when quoted outside its table header.

## Selected remediation scope

On 2026-09-11 the owner selected full uniform assurance, the recommended path:

1. confirm the corrected dependency-driven EP-D02 queue one choice at a time
   without changing the accepted rule unless a replacement is selected and
   later accepted;
2. confirm the nine EP-D07 product choices on the same basis;
3. repair R9.4 through its prerequisite-ordered atomic choices; and
4. resume corrected R10 at R10.3.

EP-D02-C1-A through C3b-A confirmed the current personal Capacity,
sixteen-host, gear-independent Bound Soul role, and item-contained legacy-
vocabulary directions without changing the accepted rule. A rejected draft
exposed that C3 was not atomic; its first replacement had six baseline choices
on the accepted branch. The later owner-written C3c replacement splits that
branch and creates the conditional system-design choices mapped above.

EP-D02-C3c.135 asked whether a fixed context/receipt tag may commit again while
its authored relationship remains continuously sufficient. The owner selected
A, so `R^{hold}_v` is empty. Every tag may commit at most once during one
continuous-sufficiency interval; any later same-tag result requires an
authoritative relationship-insufficient state followed by renewed sufficiency
and a newly authorized occurrence. Context/tag termination counts as
insufficiency, while polling, callbacks, duplicate settlement, replay, and
observation do not. The choice is necessary break topology, not a guarantee
that any tag can ever commit twice.

The selected branch retains `9/23/138`, four represented IDs, eight lived
edges, four complete IDs, no incomplete represented ID, Souls
`{6,9,10,12,14,15}`, and degree six. It still permits a persistent or channeled
payoff from the first receipt. At the same Soul/same Relic/same relationship
across the post-commit interval, the marginal break requirement is **neutral/
protective**, the inherited first-commit relation may remain **direct**, and
universal prevalence is **aggregate**. C46-A's same-boundary width remains two,
recurrence does not collapse exact `bound_v`, and C133/C134 apply to every later
newly authorized occurrence.

EP-D02-C3c.136 asked whether post-break rearm is a real same-encounter
capability for every tag, no tag, or a proper subset. The owner selected A, so
`K^{enc-rearm}_v=K^{candidate}_v`: every tag has at least one fully specified,
nonforked, fixed-version encounter history with initial commit `c1`, a later
authoritative relationship-insufficient state, renewed sufficiency plus
recurrence authorization, and distinct same-tag commit `c2` before terminal
encounter resolution. This guarantees capability somewhere, not a repeat after
every break or in every encounter.

The selected branch retains `9/23/138`, four represented IDs, eight lived

same tag across first commit, loss, renewal, and second commit, continuity with

EP-D02-C3c.137-A is now selected. `F^0_v` is empty: every legal adjacent
same-tag recommit path contains a material rearm opportunity after `c1` and
before the second provisional-completion cut `q2`, while the relationship is
insufficient. The actual chosen continuation need not cause renewal, and the
decision need not belong to the Relic owner.

An adversarial wording audit caught one laundering path before this result was
advanced. Generic timing or reachability could have let concede versus continue
or a long unrelated action versus a short one qualify. The corrected C137
instruction requires the isolated alternatives to change a relationship-read
`H`/`S` fact, the fixed context's operative availability, or the tag-specific
recurrence gate before terminal resolution. Generic action duration, phase
advancement, encounter termination, and globally making every result
unreachable do not qualify alone. This implements the already-presented rule
that unrelated decisions do not count; it chooses no actor, source, duration,
or chosen rebuild action.

EP-D02-C3c.138-A is now selected. Fixed context `h` is operatively available at
a semantic cut when its fixed input contract remains legally instantiated and
can evaluate its relationship without first requiring a separate equip, summon,
unsuppress, rebind, recreate, or equivalent restoration operation. Runtime-
object and polling details do not decide availability.

A C137-compliant witness is context-detached when every material rearm
opportunity before `q2` occurs while `h` is unavailable, none occurs while the
same live `h` has an insufficient relationship, and that exact `h` later
returns and commits the same tag as `c2`. Let `F^{ctx-out}_v` contain tags with
at least one such witness. The selected A makes that set empty: every legal
recommit path must expose at least one relevant choice while the same context
remains operative and relationship-insufficient.

The separator is a Silenced R17. While its context is absent, Cleanse versus
another action materially changes whether it returns; it returns with Guard +
Heat already sufficient and P recommits. A rejects that restoration as the
complete reset: some material opportunity must occur while the same R17 is
live and false after `c1` and before `q2`, either before suppression or after
restoration. In this separator none occurs before Silence, so one is required
afterward. A's strongest cost is the possible double tax imposed on a deliberate,
expensive remanifestation mechanic when no earlier live-context opportunity
exists.

At the local live-context opportunity, continuing context identity and
genuinely different relationship truth coexist **directly**. The complete
loss-to-recommit trajectory remains **partial**, A's exclusion is **neutral/
protective**, and every prevalence/coexistence topology is **aggregate**.
Negative membership requires complete reachable-domain analysis. Every branch
retains `9/23/138`, four represented IDs, eight lived edges, four complete IDs,
no incomplete represented ID, Souls `{6,9,10,12,14,15}`, and degree six.

C138 does not require the context to remain continuously available from `c1`
to `q2`; temporary absence remains legal when a live-context insufficient

source atom or reuse, relationship operator, `H` retention, loss duration,

EP-D02-C3c.139-A is now selected. C138-A says that a material

matched pair keeping the context live in both arms can change a predicate-read

For a legal adjacent-commit witness `w`, `D^{live}(w)` is the nonempty set of

opportunity has `L^{live}` when a reachable isolation pair holds all later

and can thereby change whether or when renewed sufficiency plus authorization
produces `q2`. A transient exit and return before `tau` fails; generic delay or
phase divergence is insufficient. Later context loss remains legal, and the
chosen continuation need not exercise the leverage.

Let `F^{no-live-int}_v` contain tags with at least one legal witness whose every

The owner selected EP-D02-C3c.141-A. A C140-qualified actual selected action

The same-opportunity unit is one authoritative pre-choice snapshot and one

For a dual tag `k`, legal history `w`, and live joint opportunity `d`, let

occurrence becomes provisionally complete at `q2`. Exact future inputs,
success, `q2` timing, payoff, optimality,
internal variables, and the singleton hinge identity need not be revealed.
Define

`K^{pre-form}_v={F in {P,R} : some legal joint opportunity has
Lambda_F(w,d) nonempty}`.

A, recommended, makes `K^{pre-form}_v={P,R}`. Each form has some truthful
pre-choice witness, but the witnesses may use different tags, histories,
opportunities, or pairs. At each qualifying pre-choice boundary, continuing
operative context and one real causal distinction coexist **directly**; its
later relationship to `q2` and the hinge is **partial**; excluding total form
opacity is **neutral/protective**; and catalog two-form support is only
**aggregate**. This turns the bond-versus-manifestation distinction into some
player-semantic vocabulary, but a buried P reference on one obscure tag and a
buried R reference on another can satisfy A. It guarantees neither
co-legibility nor fun.

B makes `|K^{pre-form}_v|=1` and deliberately leaves its polarity for a
dependent choice. Its strongest case is an asymmetric mystery grammar: the
player may knowingly repair the relationship while manifestation permission
remains occult, or knowingly invoke while the changing bond remains
experiential. The supported form has A's **direct** local fit; the absent form
is **neutral/unsupported** at the player-information boundary; and their
asymmetry is **aggregate**. Costs are guide dependence, one strategically
opaque causal axis, and an unintended primary/secondary reading.

C makes `K^{pre-form}_v` empty. No qualifying predicate or recurrence pair is
truthfully form-distinguishable to its command-holder before commitment.
C44-A still requires the committed result identity to be learnable and later
post-choice discovery remains possible. This gives the strongest occult-Relic
experience, but player-facing causal distinction is **neutral/unsupported**,
universal opacity is **aggregate**, and blind first-use decisions, external-
guide advantage, and opaque balance feedback become systemic.

A/B/C are exhaustive over the two-element support set: both, exactly one, or
neither. A needs one explicit canonical-information witness per form; the two
may coincide. B needs one positive witness and exhaustive negative proof for
the absent form. C needs exhaustive negative proof for both. Incomplete
information analysis is unresolved.

Names, icons, colors, rarity labels, flavor, generic “affects the Relic” text,
aggregate success/failure, post-choice-only VFX or replay, debug state, data
mining, external guides, and another seat's uncommunicated information cannot
launder pre-choice legibility. A fused “does both” label proves neither form
unless the legal substitution and form-specific intervention are intelligible.
A reachable in-game tutorial or reference may count; combat-HUD presence,
discoverability, and ease remain later.

C156 adds no tag, history, opportunity, pair, continuation, cut, receipt-width,

C156 does not choose B's polarity; per-tag, per-history, per-path, or per-

chooser, authorized information, payoff/objective, and comparison horizon are
defined.

The owner selected EP-D02-C3c.156-A. Therefore `K^{pre-form}_v={P,R}`: the

The witnesses may belong to different tags, histories, opportunities, pairs,
or lawfully human-controllable roles. At each qualifying pre-choice boundary,

A frontier correction is required before the dependent two-form-overlap card.
C143 could classify mechanical per-tag overlap immediately because C141-A had
already made every tag's mechanical form-support set nonempty. C156-A makes
only the **catalog union** `{P,R}` nonempty and permits different witness tags;
it does not prevent a dual tag from exposing neither form. Asking “both versus
not both” now would collapse singleton-supported and wholly opaque tags into
one negative class. Any-form per-tag coverage is therefore the coarser missing
atom.

EP-D02-C3c.157 is now the sole active owner choice. For each
`k in D^{dual}_v`, define

`K^{pre-form}_v(k)={F in {P,R} : there exist legal w,d for k with

and

`E^{pre+}_v={k in D^{dual}_v : K^{pre-form}_v(k) is nonempty}`.

C156-A makes `E^{pre+}_v` nonempty and makes the union of its per-tag support

A, recommended, makes `E^{pre+}_v=D^{dual}_v`. Every mechanically dual tag

controllable choice. It does not require both. At each witness, continuing

B makes `E^{pre+}_v` a nonempty proper subset of `D^{dual}_v`. At least one

This is the strongest occult-archetype countercase: teachable covenants can

retain A's **direct** local fit; an opaque tag's mechanical forms remain

information taxonomy, guide/data-mining advantage, blind first-use decisions,
and likely clarity or power premiums.

One positive witness uses one semantic opportunity, actual command-holder role

A, recommended, makes `E^{pre-both-op}_v=D^{dual}_v`. Every mechanically dual

opportunity `(k,w,d)`, let `A^-_u(w,d)` be the complete set of canonical,
versioned player-semantic accounts authorized inside `O^-_u(w,d)`. An account
has one authored causal scope and provenance independent of localization,
layout, asset duplication, page breaks, or rendering. One account may render
through multiple explicitly linked views; placing independent accounts in one
page or panel does not merge them. A neutral baseline may supply legal-arm
identities and already-defined general vocabulary but no target-specific P/R
mapping.

Let `Lambda_F(w,d|xi)` contain qualifying form-`F` pairs made intelligible by
that baseline plus account `xi` alone. Define

`Gamma^{pre-int}_v(w,d)={(xi,pi_P,pi_R) :
xi in A^-_u(w,d), pi_P in Lambda_P(w,d|xi),
pi_R in Lambda_R(w,d|xi), pi_P != pi_R,
and xi explicitly presents those interventions as the distinct predicate and
recurrence faces of the same continuing tag and atomic choice}`,

`J^{pre-int}(w,d) iff J^{pre-both-op}(w,d)
and Gamma^{pre-int}_v(w,d) is nonempty`,

`W^{pre-int}_v(k)={w in W_v(k) : some d in D^{joint}(w)
has J^{pre-int}(w,d)}`,

and

`E^{pre-int}_v={k in D^{dual}_v : W^{pre-int}_v(k) is nonempty}`.

This is semantic integration rather than physical colocation. One versioned
account may render through tooltip, codex, audio, or linked views; one asset or
screen may contain separate accounts. C165-A guarantees that the whole `O^-`
makes both forms legible at every joint opportunity, not that any one account
is jointly sufficient.

A, recommended, makes `E^{pre-int}_v=D^{dual}_v`. Every dual tag has at least
one exact history and joint opportunity with one authorized semantic account
that explains a qualifying P pair and distinct R pair as two faces of the same
continuing Soul–Relic choice. At the exact `(k,w,d,u,O^-_u,xi)` boundary,
continuing relationship unity and both distinctions coexist **directly**;
their later relation to `q2`, effects, and payoff is **partial**; rejecting
fused or merely colocated pseudo-integration is **neutral/protective**; and
universal tag participation is **aggregate**. Costs are synthesis,
localization, and validation per tag, denser explanations, repetitive “two
faces” boilerplate, less interpretive mystery, and token compliance through
one obscure integrated account per tag.

B makes `E^{pre-int}_v` empty. Both forms remain intelligible at every joint
choice through C165-A, but no account simultaneously supplies both exact form
accounts and explicitly relates them as two faces. A jointly sufficient account
with unrelated clauses, or a relational overview lacking both exact pair
mappings, remains integration-negative. Separate concise bond/predicate and
recurrence/permission modules are the strongest countercase: they preserve
reusable teaching and reduce dense-explanation, maintenance, and localization
coupling. Each form is **direct** in its own module; cross-account unity is
**partial**; explicit integration is **neutral/unsupported**; modularity may be
**neutral/protective**; and universal integration-negative topology is
**aggregate**. Costs are split attention and memory, weaker thematic synthesis,
apparent fragmentation, and reliance on player inference.

C makes `E^{pre-int}_v` nonempty proper. Integrated and integration-negative
dual tags coexist. Local cases inherit A/B's ratings and coexistence is
**aggregate**. Costs are another information dialect, unequal learning burden,
clarity or interpretive-flexibility premium risk, and token class membership.

A/B/C are exhaustive because `E^{pre-int}_v` is a subset of the nonempty dual
domain: whole, empty, or nonempty proper. A separate catalog-existence card is
unnecessary because B covers global absence and C covers nonempty nonuniversal
participation. C165-A prevents a negative tag from hiding either form; negative
means only that no integrated account exists. Same-reference and same-screen
states create no fourth class because they remain orthogonal later axes.

A requires one exact `(w,d,u,O^-_u,xi,pi_P,pi_R)` witness for every dual tag.
B requires exhaustive analysis of every dual tag, legal history, joint opportunity,
qualifying P/R pair combination, and canonical authorized pre-choice account,
proving no single account is jointly sufficient and integrating. C combines a
positive tag with one exhaustively integration-negative tag. Failure to find a
shared tooltip, reference, or panel is not negative proof; incomplete account
or content enumeration remains unresolved.

Same-`O^-` co-legibility is not integration. A shared `referenceId`, asset,
page, menu, heading, or simultaneous screen is insufficient without one
canonical account's cross-form semantic relation. A generic “does both,”
visual adjacency, icon/color similarity, two links under one heading, or
player inference cannot manufacture an integrated witness. Different
renderings do not split one versioned account; arbitrary concatenation does
not create one. Generated text counts only when its canonical grammar emits a
truthful integrated account for the target arms. One bundled pair cannot
satisfy both forms, and aliases count once.

No cross-opportunity, history, tag, role, seat, version, or post-commit
stitching is allowed. Guides, private AI state, post-choice feedback, data
mining, and uncommunicated other-seat information remain invalid. A stable
generic account may support multiple tags only when truthful for each exact
arm set; those are separate indexed witnesses.

Every branch inherits C165-A's same-role two-form co-legibility and C164-A's
lawfully human-controllable observation contract. C166 adds no controller
count or identity and does not require actual human occupancy. B/C cannot
distribute form-legibility evidence across players; both forms remain within
the same command-holder's `O^-`, and only their semantic provenance differs.

A retains the tight three-tag construction and tightly uses one dual tag-
history, one co-legible joint opportunity, one integrated account-witness
incidence, two form memberships, and two legible-pair incidences. B retains the
same three tags, history, opportunity, memberships, and pairs but has zero
integrated witnesses. C requires two dual tags plus both specialists, giving a
tight four-tag floor and, tightly, two tag-history incidences, two co-legible
opportunities, one integrated witness, four form memberships, and four legible-
pair incidences.

Every joint opportunity has at least two mechanical pairs and three semantic
continuations. A/B/C therefore derive `2/2/4` opportunity-indexed mechanical-
pair incidences and `3/3/6` continuation incidences. No branch adds an
opportunity, receipt, result width, cut, definition, exact key, Soul row,
reference identity, or simultaneous surface. All retain nine definitions,
twenty-three exact keys, 138 Soul/key rows at six Souls, four represented
locked IDs, eight lived aligned edges, four one-history-complete IDs, no
incomplete represented ID, Souls `{6,9,10,12,14,15}`, degree six, and C46-A's
same-boundary width two.

C166 defers class identities and prevalence; per-history and every-opportunity
integration saturation; reference-asset identity; simultaneous presentation;
account counts, salience, order, modality, accessibility, and wording;
command-holder identity, controller and communication; hinge disclosure;
timing, feedback, value, non-dominance, payoff, power, fun, UI, release, and
implementation. Under A, per-history integrated-account saturation is the
next dependent breadth atom. Under B/C, recompute the applicable branch. C166
was the sole active owner choice at presentation.

The owner selected EP-D02-C3c.166-A. Therefore
`E^{pre-int}_v=D^{dual}_v`: every mechanically dual tag has at least one exact
legal history and joint opportunity with one canonical authorized semantic
account that makes a qualifying P pair and distinct R pair intelligible and
explicitly relates them as two faces of the same continuing Soul–Relic choice.
At `(k,w,d,u,O^-_u,xi)`, unity and the two real distinctions coexist
**directly**; their later relation to `q2`, effects, and payoff remains
**partial**; excluding fused or merely colocated pseudo-integration is
**neutral/protective**; and universal tag participation is **aggregate**.
C166-A does not require integration on every history or opportunity,
persistence of one account or reference, or one simultaneous presentation. It
raises no inherited catalog or mechanical floor, but adds a tight minimum of
one integrated-account-witness incidence: three tags, one dual tag-history, one
co-legible joint opportunity, two form memberships, two legible pairs, two
mechanical pairs, and three continuations otherwise remain tight. The authoritative
decision record remains unchanged.

EP-D02-C3c.167 is now the sole active owner choice. Retain
`W^{pre-int}_v(k)` and define

`W^{pre-int0}_v(k)=W_v(k) setminus W^{pre-int}_v(k)`

and

`U^{pre-all-int}_v={k in D^{dual}_v :
W^{pre-int}_v(k)=W_v(k)}`.

An integration-negative history has `Gamma^{pre-int}_v(w,d)` empty at every
joint opportunity. C165-A still gives the same actual command-holder distinct
legible P and R pairs at every such opportunity. Negative means only that no
one canonical account at a target choice both supplies the exact mappings and
explicitly relates them as two faces; separate modules, unrelated joint
clauses, and relational overviews missing an exact mapping remain negative.

A, recommended, makes `U^{pre-all-int}_v=D^{dual}_v`. Every exact legal
history of every dual tag has at least one joint opportunity with an integrated
account. Accounts, opportunities, pairs, wording, and rendering may differ
between histories, and other opportunities may remain negative. Each exact
account boundary is **direct**; continuity across histories is **partial**;
excluding wholly negative histories is **neutral/protective**; and universal
history/tag saturation is **aggregate**. This closes C166-A's obscure-history
loophole while preserving modular teaching elsewhere. Costs are history-wide
proof, synthesis/localization, repetition, less route-specific mystery, and
one-buried-account token compliance.

B makes `U^{pre-all-int}_v` empty. Every dual tag has C166-A's positive history
and a distinct integration-negative history. Its strongest case is biography-
sensitive initiation: one route explicitly names the unity while another gives
complete modular truths and lets play or interpretation synthesize them.
Positive accounts remain **direct**; both form comparisons on a negative
history remain individually **direct** within the same `O^-` while explicit
integration is **neutral/unsupported**; same-tag continuity across histories is
**partial**;
deliberate mystery may be **neutral/protective**; and universal mixing is
**aggregate**. Costs are route-dependent comprehension, guide advantage,
apparent inconsistency, and token positive or negative histories.

C makes `U^{pre-all-int}_v` a nonempty proper subset. History-saturated and
history-mixed dual tags coexist. Local cases inherit A/B's ratings and catalog
coexistence is **aggregate**. Costs are another information dialect, uneven
learning burden, clarity or interpretive-flexibility premiums, saturation and
negative-proof burdens, and token class membership.

A/B/C are exhaustive because C166-A makes every tag's positive-history set
nonempty. A tag is saturated or has both a positive and a negative history;
the saturated subset is whole, empty, or nonempty proper. A never-integrated
tag would reopen C166-A.

A requires an integrated witness on every exact `(k,w)`. B requires, for each
tag, the inherited positive history and one distinct history exhaustively
negative across every joint opportunity, account, and qualifying P/R pair
combination. C combines those proof modes. One nonintegrating account or one

enumeration remains unresolved. Accounts cannot be stitched across choices or
histories. A reusable account counts on each history only when authorized in
that target choice's exact `O^-` and truthful for its exact legal arms.
Reference, asset, page, screen, heading, and simultaneous-presentation identity
remain neither necessary nor sufficient.

The tight A/B/C indexed floors are respectively: three candidate tags, one

represented locked IDs, eight lived aligned edges, four one-history-complete

EP-D02-C3c.168 is now the sole active owner choice. For every

B makes `U^{pre-all-op-int}_v` empty. Every dual tag has at least one exact

deliberate modular pacing may be **neutral/protective**, and universal mixing

C makes `U^{pre-all-op-int}_v` a nonempty proper subset. Opportunity-saturated
and opportunity-mixed dual tags coexist. Local cases inherit A/B's ratings and

proof modes, and token class membership.

A/B/C are exhaustive: every history has a positive opportunity, so a dual tag
is opportunity-saturated or has at least one history containing a positive

A requires an integrated witness on every exact `(k,w,d)`. B requires, for

Every branch inherits same-role two-form co-legibility and the lawfully human-

**Frontier correction:** the prior pointer to cross-opportunity account

history contains exactly one joint opportunity, making reuse vacuous or

EP-D02-C3c.169 is now the sole active owner choice. Define

`W^{2j}_v(k)={w in W_v(k) : |D^{joint}(w)|>=2}`

and

A, recommended, makes `E^{2j}_v=D^{dual}_v`. Every dual tag has at least one
exact legal history with at least two distinct mechanically joint
opportunities; other histories may remain singleton. C168-A's local

participation across the distinct choices remains **partial** because account
identity and cross-choice mechanics remain open; excluding counterfeit
multiplicity is **neutral/protective**; and universal tag participation is

B makes `E^{2j}_v` empty. Since C150-A makes every `D^{joint}(w)` nonempty,

is one dense, memorable integrated ritual per biography rather than recurring
Relic maintenance. The sole choice retains C168-A's **direct** fit; cross-
opportunity participation is **neutral/unsupported**; structural simplicity
may be **neutral/protective**; and universal singleton topology is
**aggregate**. Costs are no within-history recurring joint relationship, no
nonvacuous account-reuse play, and less room for the two forms to evolve across

C makes `E^{2j}_v` a nonempty proper subset. Multi-opportunity-capable and

and authoring burden, agency/power premiums, exhaustive singleton proof, and
token positive membership.

Example A: one Ashen Mercy history contains distinct Tend and Manifest

choice.

A/B/C are exhaustive. C150-A gives every legal dual history a nonempty

A requires, per dual tag, one exact legal `w` and distinct

EP-D02-C3c.170 is now the sole active owner choice. Because C166's `xi` is a
canonical, versioned semantic account, equality below follows authored causal
scope and provenance rather than raw storage ID, wording, localization,
reference asset, layout, or rendering. Define

`I^{pre-int}_v(k,w,d)={xi : there exist pi_P,pi_R with
(xi,pi_P,pi_R) in Gamma^{pre-int}_v(w,d)}`,

`P^{pre-reuse}_v(k,w)={{d_i,d_j} subseteq D^{joint}(w) :
d_i != d_j and I^{pre-int}_v(k,w,d_i) intersect
I^{pre-int}_v(k,w,d_j) is nonempty}` for `w in W^{2j}_v(k)`,

`W^{pre-reuse}_v(k)={w in W^{2j}_v(k) :
P^{pre-reuse}_v(k,w) is nonempty}`,

and

W^{pre-reuse}_v(k) is nonempty}`.

The positive operative relation is one exact
`(k,w,{d_i,d_j},xi)`: two commitment-separated joint choices on one legal
history at which the same canonical account independently satisfies
`Gamma^{pre-int}`. Their exact P/R pairs may differ.

A, recommended, makes `E^{pre-reuse}_v=D^{dual}_v`. Every dual tag has at
least one exact history and one distinct opportunity pair sharing a canonical
integrated-account identity; other histories and pairs may remain reuse-
negative. One learned relational grammar can return as the same covenant in a
new tactical expression. Local C168 integration remains **direct**. The same
account's participation across two commitment-separated choices is
**partial**, not a new same-boundary direct realization; lived recognition is
likewise **partial** because presentation sameness and actual notice are not
required; anti-aliasing is **neutral/protective**; and universal per-tag
participation is **aggregate**. Costs are repetition, generic-account
pressure, stale-context risk, coupled maintenance/versioning, and token
compliance through an obscure reused pair. Example: one Ashen Mercy history's
Tend and later Manifest opportunities both authorize `Covenant–Echo v3`,
which truthfully maps each choice's potentially different exact P/R pairs.

B makes `E^{pre-reuse}_v` empty. On every legal history of every dual tag, the
qualifying integrated-account sets of every two distinct joint opportunities
are disjoint. C169-A supplies a nonvacuous multi-opportunity history per tag;
accounts may still recur across different histories or tags. Its strongest
case is biography-specific semantic evolution rather than one catch-all
primer. Local unity/distinction remains **direct**; same-tag/history mechanical
continuity remains **partial**; same-account continuity is
**neutral/unsupported**; protecting genuine semantic change may be
**neutral/protective**; and universal separation is **aggregate**. Costs are
relearning, authored-content and validation burden, terminology drift, weaker
recognizable continuity, and cosmetic-alias novelty. Example: Tend authorizes

C makes `E^{pre-reuse}_v` a nonempty proper subset. Reuse-capable and same-
history reuse-negative dual tags coexist. Local cases inherit A/B's ratings
and class coexistence is **aggregate**. Costs are another account-continuity

exhaustive negative proof, and token positive membership. Example: Ashen
reuses `Covenant–Echo v3`; every Dreamglass multi-opportunity history uses
pairwise-disjoint qualifying integrated accounts.

A/B/C are exhaustive. C169-A makes `W^{2j}_v(k)` nonempty for every dual tag,
and C168-A makes each opportunity's `I^{pre-int}` nonempty. A tag therefore has
some same-history opportunity pair with nonempty account-identity intersection
or none; `E^{pre-reuse}_v` is whole, empty, or nonempty proper.

A requires, per dual tag, one exact `w,d_i,d_j,xi` and a separate
`Gamma^{pre-int}` witness at each opportunity proving that the same `xi`
qualifies at both. B requires exhaustive enumeration of every multi-
opportunity history, unordered opportunity pair, canonical account identity,
and qualifying mapping, proving all intersections empty. C combines one A-
style tag and one distinct exhaustively negative tag. Incomplete enumeration
remains unresolved.

Same reference, page, panel, string, icon, narrator, template, UI position, or
equivalent prose is not canonical identity. An unversioned alias cannot hide
changed scope or provenance. The same account merely present at both choices
does not count unless it independently integrates both exact forms at both;
P at one and R at another cannot be stitched. Different histories, reloads,
replays, branch-exclusive choices, player memory, generic primers, and neutral
baselines cannot manufacture reuse. Rendering differences do not defeat reuse
when one exact canonical account remains truthful and qualifying at both.

C170-A requires one edge only. Sets `{a},{a},{b}` satisfy it without every
opportunity pair overlapping. Sets `{a,b}`, `{b,c}`, and `{a,c}` make every
pair overlap while having no whole-history common identity. C170 therefore
does not choose all-pairs overlap, graph connectedness, or common-core reuse.

Tight coattaining indexed incidences are:

| Branch | Candidate tags | Dual `(k,w)` incidences | Joint opportunities | Integrated witnesses | Reuse-positive / negative opportunity pairs | Minimum distinct canonical integrated-account identities | Joint-opportunity form memberships / legible pairs | Joint-opportunity mechanical pairs / continuations |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| A | 3 | 1 | 2 | 2 | 1 / 0 | 1 | 4 / 4 | 4 / 6 |
| B | 3 | 1 | 2 | 2 | 0 / 1 | 2 | 4 / 4 | 4 / 6 |
| C | 4 | 2 | 4 | 4 | 1 / 1 | 2 globally | 8 / 8 | 8 / 12 |

The counts are scoped to the listed dual joint opportunities. They coattain;
C uses identity `a` twice on its positive tag and `a`,`b` on its negative tag,
so permitted cross-tag reuse keeps the global identity floor at two. A adds no
C169 indexed or mechanical floor. B derives two identities within a qualifying
two-opportunity history. C requires two dual tags plus both specialists, two
multi-opportunity histories, and four joint opportunities. No branch raises
local width or adds a result, receipt, cut, definition, exact key, Soul row,
reference asset, or simultaneous presentation surface. Every branch retains
nine definitions, twenty-three exact keys, 138 Soul/key rows at six Souls,
four represented locked IDs, eight lived aligned edges, four one-history-
complete IDs, no incomplete represented ID, Souls `{6,9,10,12,14,15}`, degree
six, and C46-A's width two.

C170 defers tag identities/prevalence, reuse on every multi-opportunity
history, every-opportunity participation, all-pairs overlap, whole-history
common identity, graph topology, exact account counts, pair/arm identity,
continuous availability, reference/UI identity, presentation and recognition,
controller/player/role identity and communication, ordering, cadence,
strategic distinction, non-dominance, payoff, power, fun, release, migration,
and implementation. A guarantees authorized availability, not that a player
opens, notices, or remembers the account. Under A, per-multi-history reuse
saturation is next. Under B/C, recompute the branch frontier. C170 is the sole
active owner choice.

The owner selected EP-D02-C3c.170-A. Therefore
`E^{pre-reuse}_v=D^{dual}_v`: every dual tag has at least one exact legal
history and one pair of distinct, commitment-separated joint opportunities
whose qualifying integrated-account sets share one exact canonical identity.
The same `xi` independently satisfies `Gamma^{pre-int}` at both choices;
their P/R pairs, arms, wording, references, and renderings may differ. Other
histories and pairs may remain reuse-negative. Local C168 integration remains
**direct**. Participation of one account across temporal commitments is
**partial**, truthful identity and anti-aliasing are **neutral/protective**,
and universal tag participation is **aggregate**. The tight construction
remains three candidate tags, one dual multi-opportunity history, two joint
opportunities, two integrated witnesses, one reuse-positive pair, one
canonical identity, four form memberships, four legible pairs, four
mechanical pairs, and six continuations. The authoritative decision record
remains unchanged.

EP-D02-C3c.171 was drafted as the next owner choice, then reclassified by the
master closure index as `AUTHOR/EVALUATE`, not `OWNER`. Its prevalence branches
remain below as preserved analysis; none is selected. C170-A is sufficient for
the framework's existential continuity promise. Retain the C169-C170 sets and
define

`W^{pre-reuse0}_v(k)=W^{2j}_v(k) setminus W^{pre-reuse}_v(k)`

and

`U^{pre-all-2j-reuse}_v={k in D^{dual}_v :
W^{pre-reuse}_v(k)=W^{2j}_v(k)}`.

A, recommended, makes `U^{pre-all-2j-reuse}_v=D^{dual}_v`: every legal
history of every dual tag that contains at least two joint opportunities has
some opportunity pair sharing a qualifying canonical integrated-account
identity. Singleton histories remain legal and outside the denominator. B
makes every dual tag history-mixed: C170-A's reuse-positive history coexists
with a distinct reuse-negative multi-opportunity history. C makes a nonempty
class of saturated tags coexist with a distinct history-mixed tag class.

A/B/C are exhaustive after C170-A because each tag's nonempty
`W^{pre-reuse}_v(k)` either equals its nonempty `W^{2j}_v(k)` or is a proper
subset, and the saturated set is whole, empty, or nonempty proper. A requires
one exact reuse witness on every enumerated multi-opportunity history. B/C
also require exhaustive negative proof over every opportunity pair and
qualifying canonical account on the designated negative histories.

C171 does not choose whether every history is multi-opportunity; exact
history, opportunity, edge, or account counts; every-opportunity
participation; connectedness; all-pairs overlap; one whole-history common
account; presentation or recognition; strategic value, power, fun, UI,
release, migration, or implementation. Under A, a history with at least three
joint opportunities is the prerequisite next atom. With only two vertices,
one reuse edge automatically gives vertex coverage, connectedness, all-pairs
overlap, and a common account, so those properties cannot yet be separated.
Under B/C, recompute the branch frontier.

The lean alternative would have trusted
the complete accepted replays for EP-D02 and EP-D07 and repaired only R9.4; it
was not selected, and it would not have proved that every historical choice
received the later one-at-a-time treatment.
